# Copied files and their checksums

Every file below was copied byte for byte from the Netgen Admin UI bundle (se7enxweb/admin-ui-bundle,
GPL-2.0-or-later) and the libraries it ships, as installed on the Nexus v2 reference site. The sha256
is the checksum of the source. "copy" means the file in design/adminui is byte-identical to it; "changed" means
it was ported or merged with Exponential's later changes (see merging.md), so only the source matches the sum.
Source roots: legacy design = `bundle/ezpublish_legacy/ngadminui/design/ngadminui/`, bundle css/js/fonts/
images = `bundle/Resources/public/`, Font Awesome, Bootstrap and Ace = `bundle/Resources/public/bower/`.

Not copied as they are (they need YUI, which Exponential 6 no longer ships): children.tpl, content/edit.tpl and
tags/dashboard.tpl are ported by hand without YUI, the others are served by admin4 (see merging.md):

- `javascript/ezajaxsubitems_datatable.js`
- `templates/children.tpl`
- `templates/children_detailed.tpl`
- `templates/content/datatype/edit/ezdate.tpl`
- `templates/content/datatype/edit/ezdatetime.tpl`
- `templates/content/datatype/edit/ezobjectrelation_ajaxuploader.tpl`
- `templates/content/datatype/edit/ezobjectrelationlist_ajaxuploader.tpl`
- `templates/content/edit.tpl`
- `templates/tags/dashboard.tpl`
